```
     1→# Claude Gateway: Multi-Provider Model Support via LiteLLM
     2→
     3→**Date:** 2026-02-26
     4→**Status:** Approved
     5→
     6→## Problem
     7→
     8→The application is locked to Anthropic models. Users who want to use OpenAI, Google Gemini, Mistral, or other providers must wait for native support or switch tools entirely.
     9→
    10→## Solution
    11→
    12→Embed a LiteLLM-based translation layer in the existing credential proxy (`proxy.py`). When the Claude Agent SDK makes API calls through the proxy, the gateway detects whether the target model is Anthropic or non-Anthropic and routes accordingly:
    13→
    14→- **Anthropic models** -> forward to `api.anthropic.com` (existing behavior, unchanged)
    15→- **Non-Anthropic models** -> use LiteLLM to translate the Anthropic Messages API request to the target provider's format, call the provider, and translate the response back to Anthropic format
    16→
    17→The sidecar, Claude Agent SDK, frontend, and agent logic remain completely untouched.
    18→
    19→```
    20→SDK -> POST /anthropic/v1/messages -> proxy.py
    21→  |-- model=claude-* -> forward to api.anthropic.com (existing)
    22→  |-- model=gpt-4o   -> gateway.py -> LiteLLM -> openai.com -> translate response -> Anthropic format
    23→```
    24→
    25→## Architecture
    26→
    27→### Model Detection & Routing
    28→
    29→A helper function `is_anthropic_model(model_name)` classifies model names:
    30→
    31→- **Anthropic:** `claude-*`, plus short aliases `haiku`, `sonnet`, `opus`
    32→- **Non-Anthropic:** everything else, using LiteLLM's naming convention (`gpt-4o`, `gemini/gemini-2.0-flash`, `mistral/mistral-large-latest`, `ollama/llama3`, etc.)
    33→
    34→Routing in `proxy.py` intercepts `POST` requests to `v1/messages`. All other paths forward to Anthropic as before, except `GET v1/models` which is augmented (see below).
    35→
    36→```python
    37→# In proxy_anthropic(), simplified:
    38→if method == "POST" and path == "v1/messages":
    39→    body = parse_json(request)
    40→    model = body.get("model", "")
    41→    if not is_anthropic_model(model):
    42→        return await gateway_handler(body, stream=body.get("stream", False))
    43→# Otherwise fall through to existing upstream forwarding
    44→```
    45→
    46→### Model Alias Resolution (`/v1/models`)
    47→
    48→The Claude Agent SDK calls `GET /v1/models` to resolve short aliases (e.g., `haiku` -> `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001`) for both orchestrator and subagent models. Non-Anthropic model names would fail this resolution.
    49→
    50→Solution: always intercept `GET /v1/models`:
    51→
    52→1. Forward to `api.anthropic.com` to get real Anthropic models
    53→2. Collect all configured non-Anthropic model names from env vars (`ANTHROPIC_MODEL`, `SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL`, `CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL`)
    54→3. Append synthetic entries for non-Anthropic models so the SDK's alias resolution accepts them
    55→
    56→This enables mixed-provider configurations:
    57→```bash
    58→ANTHROPIC_MODEL=gpt-4o                        # orchestrator on OpenAI
    59→SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL=haiku                       # subagent on Anthropic
    60→REDACTED.0-flash   # subagent on Google
    61→```
    62→
    63→### Request/Response Translation (`gateway.py`)
    64→
    65→New module `backend/app/gateway.py` handles bidirectional translation using LiteLLM.
    66→
    67→**Non-streaming flow:**
    68→
    69→1. Receive Anthropic Messages API request body (`model`, `messages`, `system`, `tools`, `max_tokens`, etc.)
    70→2. Convert to OpenAI-compatible format (LiteLLM's internal standard)
    71→3. Call `litellm.acompletion(model="gpt-4o", messages=[...], tools=[...])`
    72→4. Convert the OpenAI-format response back to Anthropic Messages API response (`content` blocks with `type: "text"` / `type: "tool_use"`, `stop_reason`, `usage`, etc.)
    73→5. Return as JSON HTTP response
    74→
    75→**Streaming flow:**
    76→
    77→1. Same request translation
    78→2. Call `litellm.acompletion(model="gpt-4o", ..., stream=True)`
    79→3. Convert each OpenAI streaming chunk to Anthropic SSE events:
    80→   - Emit `message_start` with message metadata
    81→   - Convert `choices[].delta.content` -> `content_block_delta` with `text_delta`
    82→   - Convert `choices[].delta.tool_calls` -> `content_block_start` + `content_block_delta` with `input_json_delta`
    83→   - Emit `message_delta` with `stop_reason` and final `usage`
    84→   - Emit `message_stop`
    85→4. Yield as `text/event-stream` response
    86→
    87→**Tool use round-trip (critical path):**
    88→
    89→- **Request direction:** Anthropic `tools` array (name, description, input_schema) -> OpenAI `tools` array (function name, description, parameters). Straightforward mapping.
    90→- **Response direction:** OpenAI `tool_calls` (function name, arguments as JSON string) -> Anthropic `tool_use` content blocks (name, input as parsed object, unique `id`).
    91→- **Tool results:** Anthropic `tool_result` messages (with `tool_use_id`) -> OpenAI `tool` role messages. LiteLLM handles this natively.
    92→
    93→### Configuration
    94→
    95→**Existing env vars** get broader meaning -- they now accept any LiteLLM model name:
    96→
    97→- `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` -- orchestrator model (default: `claude-sonnet-4-6`)
    98→- `SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL` -- sql analyst subagent (default: `haiku`)
    99→- `CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL` -- chart builder subagent (default: `haiku`)
   100→
   101→**New env vars** for provider API keys (LiteLLM reads these automatically from the environment):
   102→
   103→- `OPENAI_API_KEY` -- for GPT models
   104→- `GEMINI_API_KEY` -- for Google Gemini
   105→- `MISTRAL_API_KEY` -- for Mistral
   106→- `GROQ_API_KEY` -- for Groq
   107→- Any other provider key supported by LiteLLM
   108→
   109→`ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` warning in `config.py` is relaxed -- it's no longer required if using only non-Anthropic providers.
   110→
   111→## Files Changed
   112→
   113→| File | Change |
   114→|---|---|
   115→| `backend/app/gateway.py` | **New** -- LiteLLM translation layer (~250 lines) |
   116→| `backend/app/proxy.py` | Modify routing to detect non-Anthropic models and delegate to gateway; augment `/v1/models` response |
   117→| `backend/app/config.py` | Relax `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` warning |
   118→| `backend/.env.example` | Add provider key examples and non-Anthropic model examples |
   119→| `backend/pyproject.toml` | Add `litellm` dependency |
   120→
   121→**No changes to:** sidecar, frontend, agent.py, container_manager, MCP tools.
   122→
   123→## Limitations
   124→
   125→- **Anthropic-specific features** (prompt caching, extended thinking, citations, PDF input) are silently dropped for non-Anthropic models
   126→- **Tool use quality varies** across providers -- GPT-4o and Gemini handle multi-step tool chains well, smaller/open-source models may struggle
   127→- **Streaming latency** -- adds one translation hop but no extra network round-trips
   128→- **Provider-specific errors** need translation to Anthropic error format for the SDK to handle gracefully
   129→
```

Perfect! I have all the information needed. Let me provide you with a comprehensive summary:

## Backend Analysis Report

### 1. **proxy.py** (Full Content Above)

The credential proxy currently:
- Forwards all Anthropic API requests to `https://api.anthropic.com`
- Uses a single `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` from config for all requests (line 92)
- Validates session tokens for rate-limiting/security
- Skips certain request/response headers to prevent client interference
- Allows unauthenticated passthrough for read-only endpoints like `/v1/models`

**Key modification needed**: Line 92 needs to route to different API endpoints based on the model provider instead of always using `ANTHROPIC_UPSTREAM`.

### 2. **config.py** (Full Content Above)

Current configuration variables:
- `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` - currently required, issues warning if empty (line 6-13)
- `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` - default "claude-sonnet-4-6" (line 15)
- `SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL` - default "haiku" (line 16)
- `CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL` - default "haiku" (line 17)
- Langfuse credentials for tracing
- Container isolation settings
- CORS configuration

**Key modification needed**: Make `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` optional and add new provider API keys (e.g., `OPENAI_API_KEY`, `GOOGLE_API_KEY`).

### 3. **pyproject.toml** (Full Content Above)

Current dependencies:
- fastapi, uvicorn, duckdb
- claude-agent-sdk (^0.1.38)
- langfuse, langsmith
- docker for container management
- **litellm is NOT currently included**

**Key addition needed**: Add `litellm` to dependencies (line 9-20).

### 4. **gateway.py Status**

**Does NOT exist** — needs to be created as a new module.

### 5. **agent.py** - Model Usage Summary

The agent configuration is multi-model:

**Main Agent** (line 255):
- Uses `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` (default "claude-sonnet-4-6")
- Configured in payload sent to the TypeScript sidecar container

**Subagents** (lines 63-125, 261-269):
- **sql-analyst**: Uses `SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL` (default "haiku")
  - Tools: `mcp__duckdb__execute_sql`
- **chart-builder**: Uses `CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL` (default "haiku")
  - Tools: `mcp__duckdb__execute_sql`, `mcp__duckdb__render_chart`

Each `AgentDefinition` includes:
- `description` - agent purpose
- `prompt` - system prompt with table schemas
- `tools` - list of available MCP tools
- `model` - the model identifier (passed as-is to SDK)

**Current flow** (lines 251-276):
1. Builds subagent definitions with their models
2. Creates a payload dict with all agents' definitions
3. Sends to container sidecar at `{info.url}/query` with:
   - `model`: ANTHROPIC_MODEL (orchestrator)
   - `agents`: dict of {name: {model, prompt, tools, description}}
4. The TypeScript sidecar constructs agent definitions and passes them to Claude SDK

**Key insight**: Models are passed as simple strings to the SDK, which means litellm (in the gateway layer) must translate short names like "haiku" or "gpt-4-turbo" to their full provider URLs.

     1→import logging
     2→import uuid
     3→from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
     4→
     5→from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Request
     6→from fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse
     7→import httpx
     8→
     9→from app.config import ANTHROPIC_API_KEY
    10→
    11→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    12→
    13→ANTHROPIC_UPSTREAM = "https://api.anthropic.com"
    14→
    15→_SKIP_REQUEST_HEADERS = {
    16→    "host", "content-length", "transfer-encoding", "connection",
    17→    "x-api-key",         # client must not override billing identity
    18→    "anthropic-version", # proxy controls API version
    19→    # anthropic-beta is intentionally NOT blocked: Claude Code sends beta body
    20→    # fields (e.g. context_management) alongside the matching beta header, and
    21→    # Anthropic rejects those fields as "extra inputs" if the header is absent.
    22→}
    23→_SKIP_RESPONSE_HEADERS = {"transfer-encoding", "content-encoding", "connection"}
    24→
    25→
    26→class ProxyTokenStore:
    27→    def __init__(self, ttl_seconds: int = 600):
    28→        self._ttl = ttl_seconds
    29→        self._tokens: dict[str, datetime] = {}
    30→
    31→    def create_token(self) -> str:
    32→        token = str(uuid.uuid4())
    33→        self._tokens[token] = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(seconds=self._ttl)
    34→        return token
    35→
    36→    def validate_token(self, token: str) -> bool:
    37→        # Tokens are intentionally multi-use: Claude Code makes many API calls
    38→        # per session, all protected by the same UUID. The token is valid for
    39→        # the session TTL (600s safety net) but is explicitly revoked by
    40→        # proxy_token_store.revoke_token() in agent.py's finally block when
    41→        # the Claude Code subprocess exits. Do not make single-use.
    42→        expiry = self._tokens.get(token)
    43→        if expiry is None:
    44→            return False
    45→        if datetime.now(timezone.utc) > expiry:
    46→            self._tokens.pop(token, None)
    47→            return False
    48→        return True
    49→
    50→    def revoke_token(self, token: str) -> None:
    51→        self._tokens.pop(token, None)
    52→
    53→    def cleanup_expired(self) -> int:
    54→        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    55→        expired = [t for t, exp in self._tokens.items() if now > exp]
    56→        for t in expired:
    57→            del self._tokens[t]
    58→        return len(expired)
    59→
    60→
    61→proxy_token_store = ProxyTokenStore()
    62→
    63→router = APIRouter(prefix="/anthropic")
    64→
    65→
    66→# Read-only discovery endpoints that the Claude Code SDK calls during startup
    67→# (e.g. GET /v1/models to resolve short model aliases like "sonnet").
    68→# These carry no session-specific data, so we forward them with the real API
    69→# key without requiring a registered session token.
    70→_UNAUTHENTICATED_PASSTHROUGH = {"v1/models"}
    71→
    72→
    73→@router.api_route("/{path:path}", methods=["GET", "POST", "PUT", "DELETE", "PATCH"])
    74→async def proxy_anthropic(path: str, request: Request):
    75→    # Allow a small set of read-only discovery endpoints to pass through
    76→    # without a session token so the SDK can resolve short model names.
    77→    if path not in _UNAUTHENTICATED_PASSTHROUGH:
    78→        # Claude Code CLI sends the API key as x-api-key (Anthropic SDK default).
    79→        # Fall back to Authorization: Bearer for other clients.
    80→        session_token = (
    81→            request.headers.get("x-api-key")
    82→            or request.headers.get("authorization", "")[len("Bearer "):]
    83→            or None
    84→        )
    85→        if not session_token or not proxy_token_store.validate_token(session_token):
    86→            raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid or expired session token")
    87→
    88→    headers = {
    89→        k: v for k, v in request.headers.items()
    90→        if k.lower() not in _SKIP_REQUEST_HEADERS
    91→    }
    92→    headers["x-api-key"] = ANTHROPIC_API_KEY
    93→    headers["anthropic-version"] = "2023-06-01"
    94→
    95→    body = await request.body()
    96→
    97→    # Use a fresh client per request: streaming responses that are cut short
    98→    # (e.g. client disconnects) leave connections in a partial read state, and
    99→    # reusing a shared SSL context across those connections causes
   100→    # SSLV3_ALERT_BAD_RECORD_MAC / record layer failure errors.
   101→    # Query params are intentionally NOT forwarded — Claude Code appends
   102→    # internal params (e.g. ?beta=true) that Anthropic rejects as "extra inputs".
   103→    client = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(300.0))
   104→    try:
   105→        upstream_req = client.build_request(
   106→            method=request.method,
   107→            url=f"{ANTHROPIC_UPSTREAM}/{path}",
   108→            headers=headers,
   109→            content=body,
   110→        )
   111→        upstream_resp = await client.send(upstream_req, stream=True)
   112→
   113→        response_headers = {
   114→            k: v for k, v in upstream_resp.headers.items()
   115→            if k.lower() not in _SKIP_RESPONSE_HEADERS
   116→        }
   117→
   118→        async def body_generator():
   119→            try:
   120→                async for chunk in upstream_resp.aiter_bytes():
   121→                    yield chunk
   122→            finally:
   123→                await upstream_resp.aclose()
   124→                await client.aclose()
   125→
   126→        return StreamingResponse(
   127→            body_generator(),
   128→            status_code=upstream_resp.status_code,
   129→            headers=response_headers,
   130→        )
   131→    except Exception:
   132→        await client.aclose()
   133→        logger.exception("Proxy request to upstream failed: %s %s", request.method, path)
   134→        raise
   135→
```

    11→def test_create_token_returns_uuid_string():
    12→    store = ProxyTokenStore()
    13→    token = store.create_token()
    14→    parsed = uuid_module.UUID(token)  # raises ValueError if not valid UUID
    15→    assert parsed.version == 4
    16→
    17→
    18→def test_valid_token_passes_validation():
    19→    store = ProxyTokenStore()
    20→    token = store.create_token()
    21→    assert store.validate_token(token) is True
    22→
    23→
    24→def test_unknown_token_fails_validation():
    25→    store = ProxyTokenStore()
    26→    assert store.validate_token("not-a-real-token") is False
    27→
    28→
    29→def test_revoked_token_fails_validation():
    30→    store = ProxyTokenStore()
    31→    token = store.create_token()
    32→    store.revoke_token(token)
    33→    assert store.validate_token(token) is False
    34→
    35→
    36→def test_expired_token_fails_validation():
    37→    store = ProxyTokenStore()
    38→    token = store.create_token()
    39→    # Backdate expiry directly — no sleep needed
    40→    store._tokens[token] = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(seconds=1)
    41→    assert store.validate_token(token) is False
    42→
    43→
    44→def test_revoke_nonexistent_token_is_safe():
    45→    store = ProxyTokenStore()
    46→    store.revoke_token("ghost-token")  # must not raise
    47→
    48→
    49→def test_cleanup_expired_removes_stale_tokens_and_returns_count():
    50→    store = ProxyTokenStore()
    51→    live = store.create_token()
    52→    stale = store.create_token()
    53→    store._tokens[stale] = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(seconds=1)
    54→
    55→    removed = store.cleanup_expired()
    56→
    57→    assert removed == 1
    58→    assert store.validate_token(live) is True
    59→    assert store.validate_token(stale) is False
    60→
    61→
    62→def test_cleanup_expired_on_empty_store_returns_zero():
    63→    store = ProxyTokenStore()
    64→    assert store.cleanup_expired() == 0
    65→
    66→
    67→def make_proxy_app():
    68→    app = FastAPI()
    69→    app.include_router(router)
    70→    return app
    71→
    72→
    73→def test_proxy_route_rejects_missing_auth():

    75→    response = client.post("/anthropic/v1/messages", json={})
    76→    assert response.status_code == 401

    78→

    88→

  "content": "# Claude Gateway Implementation Plan\n\n> **For Claude:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task.\n\n**Goal:** Add a LiteLLM-based translation layer to the credential proxy so users can use non-Anthropic models (OpenAI, Gemini, Mistral, etc.) with the existing Claude Agent SDK.\n\n**Architecture:** When `POST /v1/messages` arrives at the proxy, a helper classifies the model name. Anthropic models forward to `api.anthropic.com` (existing behavior). Non-Anthropic models are routed to a new `gateway.py` module that uses LiteLLM to translate between Anthropic Messages API format and the target provider's format. `GET /v1/models` is augmented to include non-Anthropic model entries so the SDK's alias resolution accepts them.\n\n**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12, FastAPI, LiteLLM, httpx, pytest\n\n---\n\n### Task 1: Add `litellm` dependency\n\n**Files:**\n- Modify: `backend/pyproject.toml:9-20`\n\n**Step 1: Add litellm to pyproject.toml**\n\nIn `backend/pyproject.toml`, add `litellm` to the `[tool.poetry.dependencies]` section:\n\n```toml\n[tool.poetry.dependencies]\npython = \"^3.12\"\nfastapi = \"^0.129.0\"\nuvicorn = {extras = [\"standard\"], version = \"^0.41.0\"}\nduckdb = \"^1.4.4\"\npython-dotenv = \"^1.2.1\"\npython-multipart = \"^0.0.22\"\nclaude-agent-sdk = \"^0.1.38\"\nlangfuse = \"^3.0.0\"\nlangsmith = {extras = [\"claude-agent-sdk\", \"otel\"], version = \">=0.3.0\"}\nopenpyxl = \"^3.1.0\"\ndocker = \"^7.1.0\"\nlitellm = \"^1.72.0\"\n```\n\n**Step 2: Install dependency**\n\nRun: `cd backend && poetry lock && poetry install`\nExpected: litellm installed successfully, lock file updated\n\n**Step 3: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add backend/pyproject.toml backend/poetry.lock\ngit commit -m \"feat(gateway): add litellm dependency for multi-provider support\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 2: Create `gateway.py` — model detection helper + tests\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `backend/app/gateway.py`\n- Create: `backend/tests/test_gateway.py`\n\n**Step 1: Write the failing test for `is_anthropic_model`**\n\nCreate `backend/tests/test_gateway.py`:\n\n```python\nfrom app.gateway import is_anthropic_model\n\n\ndef test_claude_prefixed_models_are_anthropic():\n    assert is_anthropic_model(\"claude-sonnet-4-6\") is True\n    assert is_anthropic_model(\"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001\") is True\n    assert is_anthropic_model(\"claude-opus-4-6\") is True\n    assert is_anthropic_model(\"claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022\") is True\n\n\ndef test_short_aliases_are_anthropic():\n    assert is_anthropic_model(\"haiku\") is True\n    assert is_anthropic_model(\"sonnet\") is True\n    assert is_anthropic_model(\"opus\") is True\n\n\ndef test_openai_models_are_not_anthropic():\n    assert is_anthropic_model(\"gpt-4o\") is False\n    assert is_anthropic_model(\"gpt-4o-mini\") is False\n    assert is_anthropic_model(\"o1\") is False\n\n\ndef test_gemini_models_are_not_anthropic():\n    assert is_anthropic_model(\"gemini/gemini-2.0-flash\") is False\n    assert is_anthropic_model(\"gemini/gemini-1.5-pro\") is False\n\n\ndef test_mistral_models_are_not_anthropic():\n    assert is_anthropic_model(\"mistral/mistral-large-latest\") is False\n\n\ndef test_ollama_models_are_not_anthropic():\n    assert is_anthropic_model(\"ollama/llama3\") is False\n\n\ndef test_empty_string_is_anthropic_default():\n    \"\"\"Empty string should be treated as anthropic (will use default model).\"\"\"\n    assert is_anthropic_model(\"\") is True\n```\n\n**Step 2: Run test to verify it fails**\n\nRun: `cd backend && python -m pytest tests/test_gateway.py -v`\nExpected: FAIL with `ImportError: cannot import name 'is_anthropic_model'`\n\n**Step 3: Write minimal implementation**\n\nCreate `backend/app/gateway.py`:\n\n```python\n\"\"\"\nClaude Gateway: LiteLLM-based translation layer for non-Anthropic models.\n\nRoutes non-Anthropic model requests through LiteLLM, translating between\nthe Anthropic Messages API format and each provider's native format.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport uuid\n\nimport litellm\n\n_ANTHROPIC_SHORT_ALIASES = {\"haiku\", \"sonnet\", \"opus\"}\n\n\ndef is_anthropic_model(model: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Return True if model name refers to an Anthropic Claude model.\"\"\"\n    if not model:\n        return True\n    name = model.lower().strip()\n    if name.startswith(\"claude\"):\n        return True\n    if name in _ANTHROPIC_SHORT_ALIASES:\n        return True\n    return False\n```\n\n**Step 4: Run test to verify \"end_turn\",\n}\n\n\ndef translate_response_to_anthropic(openai_resp: dict, model: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Translate an OpenAI chat completion response to Anthropic Messages API format.\"\"\"\n    choice = openai_resp[\"choices\"][0]\n    message = choice[\"message\"]\n    finish_reason = choice.get(\"finish_reason\", \"stop\")\n\n    content_blocks: list[dict] = []\n\n    # Text content\n    text = message.get(\"content\")\n    if text:\n        content_blocks.append({\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": text})\n\n    # Tool calls\n    tool_calls = message.get(\"tool_calls\", [])\n    for tc in tool_calls:\n        func = tc[\"function\"]\n        try:\n            input_data = json.loads(func[\"arguments\"])\n        except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):\n            input_data = \"input\": input_data,\n        })\n\n    usage = openai_resp.get(\"usage\", {})\n\n    return {\n        \"id\": f\"msg_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:24]}\",\n        \"type\": \"message\",\n        \"role\": \"assistant\",\n        \"model\": model,\n        \"content\": content_blocks if content_blocks else [{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"\"}],\n        \"stop_reason\": _FINISH_REASON_MAP.get(finish_reason, \"end_turn\"),\n        \"stop_sequence\": None,\n        \"usage\": {\n            \"input_tokens\": usage.get(\"prompt_tokens\", 0),\n func.get(\"name\", \"\")\n                    active_tool_calls[tc_idx] = {\n                        \"id\": tool_id,\n                        \"name\": tool_name,\n                        \"block_idx\": content_block_idx,\n                    }\n\n                    yield _sse(\n                        \"content_block_start\",\n                        {\n                            \"type\": \"content_block_start\",\n                            \"index\": content_block_idx,\n                            \"content_block\": {\n                                \"type\": \"tool_use\",\n                                \"id\": tool_id,\n                                \"name\": tool_name,\n                                \"input\": {},\n                            },\n                        },\n                    )\n\n                # Stream tool arguments\n                func = tc.get(\"function\", {})\n                args_chunk = func.get(\"arguments\", \"\")\n                if args_chunk:\n                    yield _sse(\n                        \"content_block_delta\",\n                        {\n                            \"type\": \"content_block_delta\",\n                            \"index\": active_tool_calls[tc_idx][\"block_idx\"],\n                            \"delta\": {\n                                \"type\": \"input_json_delta\",\n                                \"partial_json\": args_chunk,\n                            },\n                        },\n                    )\n\n            # Finish\n            if finish_reason is not None:\n                # Close any open blocks\n                if has_emitted_text_block:\n                    yield _sse(\n                        \"content_block_stop\",\n                        {\"type\": \"content_block_stop\", \"index\": content_block_idx},\n                    )\n                for tc_info in active_tool_calls.values():\n                    yield _sse(\n                        \"content_block_stop\",\n                        {\"type\": \"content_block_stop\", \"index\": tc_info[\"block_idx\"]},\n                    )\n\n                yield _sse(\n                    \"message_delta\",\n                    {\n                        \"type\": \"message_delta\",\n                        \"delta\": {\n                            \"stop_reason\": _FINISH_REASON_MAP.get(finish_reason, \"end_turn\"),\n                            \"stop_sequence\": None,\n                        },\n                        \"usage\": {\"output_tokens\": output_tokens},\n                    },\n                )\n\n                yield _sse(\"message_stop\", {\"type\": \"message_stop\"})\n\n    return StreamingResponse(generate(), media_type=\"text/event-stream\")\n\n\ndef _sse(event_type: str, data: dict) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Format a Server-Sent Event.\"\"\"\n    return f\"event: {event_type}\\ndata: {json.dumps(data)}\\n\\n\"\n```\n\n**Step 4: Run all tests to verify they pass**\n\nRun: `cd backend && python -m pytest tests/test_gateway.py -v`\nExpected: All tests json\nimport uuid\n\nimport litellm\n\n_ANTHROPIC_SHORT_ALIASES = {\"haiku\", \"sonnet\", \"opus\"}\n\n\ndef is_anthropic_model(model: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Return True if model name refers to an Anthropic Claude model.\"\"\"\n    if not model:\n        return True\n    name = model.lower().strip()\n    if name.startswith(\"claude\"):\n        return True\n    if name in test_translate_system_prompt_as_list():\n    anthropic_body = {\n        \"model\": \"gpt-4o\",\n        \"max_tokens\": 1024,\n        \"system\": [{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"Be concise.\"}],\n        \"messages\": [{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": \"Hi\"}],\n    }\n    result = translate_request_to_openai(anthropic_body)\n    assert result[\"messages\"][0] == {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": \"Be concise.\"}\n\n\ndef test_translate_tools():\n    anthropic_body = {\n        \"model\": \"gpt-4o\",\n        \"max_tokens\": 1024,\n        \"messages\": [{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": \"Query data\"}],\n        \"tools\": [\n            {\n                \"name\": \"execute_sql\",\n                \"description\": \"Run a SQL query\",\n                \"input_schema\": {\n                    \"type\": \"object\",\n                    \"properties\": {\"query\": {\"type\": \"string\"}},\n                    \"required\": [\"query\"],\n                },\n            }\n        ],\n    }\n    result = translate_request_to_openai(anthropic_body)\n    assert len(result[\"tools\"]) == 1\n    tool = result[\"tools\"][0]\n    assert tool[\"type\"] == \"function\"\n    assert tool[\"function\"][\"name\"] == \"execute_sql\"\n    assert tool[\"function\"][\"description\"] == \"Run a SQL query\"\n    assert tool[\"function\"][\"parameters\"][\"properties\"][\"query\"][\"type\"] == \"string\"\n\n\ndef test_translate_tool_use_messages():\n    anthropic_body = {\n        \"model\": \"gpt-4o\",\n        \"max_tokens\": 1024,\n        \"messages\": [\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": \"Query data\"},\n            {\n                \"role\": \"assistant\",\n                \"content\": [\n                    {\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"Let me run that.\"},\n                    {\n                        \"type\": \"tool_use\",\n                        \"id\": \"tool_abc123\",\n                        \"name\": \"execute_sql\",\n                        \"input\": {\"query\": \"SELECT 1\"},\n                    },\n                ],\n            },\n            {\n                \"role\": \"user\",\n                \"content\": [\n                    {\n                        \"type\": \"tool_result\",\n                        \"tool_use_id\": \"tool_abc123\",\n                        \"content\": \"1\",\n                    }\n                ],\n            },\n        ],\n    }\n    result = translate_request_to_openai(anthropic_body)\n    assistant_msg = result[\"messages\"][1]\n    assert assistant_msg[\"role\"] == \"assistant\"\n    assert assistant_msg[\"content\"] == \"Let me run that.\"\n    assert len(assistant_msg[\"tool_calls\"]) == 1\n    tc = assistant_msg[\"tool_calls\"][0]\n    assert tc[\"id\"] == \"tool_abc123\"\n    assert tc[\"function\"][\"name\"] == \"execute_sql\"\n    assert json.loads(tc[\"function\"][\"arguments\"]) == {\"query\": \"SELECT 1\"}\n    tool_msg = result[\"messages\"][2]\n    assert tool_msg[\"role\"] == \"tool\"\n    assert tool_msg[\"tool_call_id\"] == \"tool_abc123\"\n    assert tool_msg[\"content\"] == \"1\"\n```\n\nAdd to `backend/app/gateway.py`:\n\n```python\ndef _convert_system_to_openai(system) -> str:\n    if isinstance(system, str):\n        return system\n    if isinstance(system, list):\n        return \"\\n\".join(block.get(\"text\", \"\") for block in system if block.get(\"type\") == \"text\")\n    return str(system)\n\n\ndef _convert_message_to_openai(msg: dict) -> list[dict]:\n    role = msg.get(\"role\", \"user\")\n    content = msg.get(\"content\", \"\")\n\n    if isinstance(content, str):\n        return [{\"role\": role, \"content\": content}]\n\n    if isinstance(content, list):\n        tool_results = [b for b in content if b.get(\"type\") == \"tool_result\"]\n        if tool_results:\n            result_msgs = []\n            for tr in tool_results:\n                tr_content = tr.get(\"content\", \"\")\n                if isinstance(tr_content, list):\n                    tr_content = \"\\n\".join(\n                        b.get(\"text\", \"\") for b in tr_content if b.get(\"type\") == \"text\"\n                    )\n                result_msgs.append({\n                    \"role\": \"tool\",\n                    \"tool_call_id\": tr[\"tool_use_id\"],\n                    \"content\": str(tr_content),\n                })\n            return result_msgs\n\n        tool_uses = [b for b in content if b.get(\"type\") == \"tool_use\"]\n        text_blocks = [b for b in content if b.get(\"type\") == \"text\"]\n        text_content = \"\\n\".join(b.get(\"text\", \"\") for b in text_blocks) if text_blocks else None\n\n        if tool_uses:\n            openai_msg: dict = {\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": text_content}\n            openai_msg[\"tool_calls\"] = [\n                {\n                    \"id\": tu[\"id\"],\n                    \"type\": \"function\",\n                    \"function\": {\n                        \"name\": tu[\"name\"],\n                        \"arguments\": json.dumps(tu[\"input\"]),\n                    },\n                }\n                for tu in tool_uses\n            ]\n            return [openai_msg]\n\n        combined = \"\\n\".join(b.get(\"text\", \"\") for b in content if b.get(\"type\") == \"text\")\n        return [{\"role\": role, \"content\": combined}]\n\n    return [{\"role\": role, \"content\": str(content)}]\n\n\ndef translate_request_to_openai(anthropic_body: dict) -> dict:\n    openai_messages: list[dict] = []\n\n    system = anthropic_body.get(\"system\")\n    if system:\n        openai_messages.append({\n            \"role\": \"system\",\n            \"content\": _convert_system_to_openai(system),\n        })\n\n    for msg in anthropic_body.get(\"messages\", []):\n        openai_messages.extend(_convert_message_to_openai(msg))\n\n    result: dict = {\n        \"model\": anthropic_body[\"model\"],\n        \"messages\": openai_messages,\n    }\n\n    if \"max_tokens\" in anthropic_body:\n        result[\"max_tokens\"] = anthropic_body[\"max_tokens\"]\n\n    tools = anthropic_body.get(\"tools\")\n    if tools:\n        result[\"tools\"] = [\n            {\n                \"type\": \"function\",\n                \"function\": {\n                    \"name\": t[\"name\"],\n                    \"description\": t.get(\"description\", \"\"),\n                    \"parameters\": t.get(\"input_schema\", {}),\n                },\n            }\n            for t in tools\n        ]\n\n    return result\n```\n\nRun: `poetry run python -m pytest tests/test_gateway.py -v`\nCommit: `git commit -m \"feat(gateway): add Anthropic-to-OpenAI request translation\"`\n\n## Task 4: Response translation (OpenAI -> Anthropic format) + tests\n\nAppend query that.\"\n    assert result[\"content\"][1][\"type\"] == \"tool_use\"\n    assert result[\"content\"][1][\"id\"] == \"call_abc\"\n    assert result[\"content\"][1][\"name\"] == \"execute_sql\"\n translate_response_to_anthropic(openai_resp, model=\"gpt-4o\")\n    assert result[\"stop_reason\"] == \"max_tokens\"\n```\n\nAdd to `backend/app/gateway.py`:\n\n```python\n_FINISH_REASON_MAP = {\n    \"stop\": \"end_turn\",\n    \"length\": \"max_tokens\",\n    \"tool_calls\": \"tool_use\",\n    \"content_filter\": \"end_turn\",\n}\n\n\ndef translate_response_to_anthropic(openai_resp: dict, model: str) -> dict:\n    choice = openai_resp[\"choices\"][0]\n    message = choice[\"message\"]\n    finish_reason = choice.get(\"finish_reason\", \"stop\")\n\n    content_blocks: list[dict] = []\n\n    text = message.get(\"content\")\n    if text:\n        content_blocks.append({\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": text})\n\n    tool_calls = message.get(\"tool_calls\", [])\n    for tc in tool_calls:\n        func = tc[\"function\"]\n        try:\n            input_data = json.loads(func[\"arguments\"])\n        except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):\n            input_data = {\"raw\": func.get(\"arguments\", \"\")}\n        content_blocks.append({\n            \"type\": \"tool_use\",\n            \"id\": tc[\"id\"],\n            \"name\": func[\"name\"],\n            \"input\": input_data,\n        })\n\n    usage = openai_resp.get(\"usage\", {})\n\n    return {\n        \"id\": f\"msg_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:24]}\",\n        \"type\": \"message\",\n        \"role\": \"assistant\",\n        \"model\": model,\n        \"content\": content_blocks if content_blocks else [{\"type\": \"text\", \"text\": \"\"}],\n        \"stop_reason\": _FINISH_REASON_MAP.get(finish_reason, \"end_turn\"),\n        \"stop_sequence\": None,\n        \"usage\": {\n            \"input_tokens\": usage.get(\"prompt_tokens\", 0),\n            \"output_tokens\": usage.get(\"completion_tokens\", 0),\n        },\n    }\n```\n\nRun: `poetry run python -m pytest tests/test_gateway.py -v`\nCommit: `git commit -m \"feat(gateway): add OpenAI-to-Anthropic response translation\"`\n\n## Before You Begin\n\nIf you have questions about the requirements, ask them now. \n\n## Your Job\n\n1. This is a very minor discrepancy -- in practice no real model name starts with `claude` without a hyphen, but technically the spec says `claude-*` (with hyphen). This is a low-risk edge case.

15 tests confirmed passing. Coverage:

- **is_anthropic_model:** 7 tests (claude-prefixed, short aliases, gpt, gemini, mistral, ollama, empty string)

### Extra features not in the spec

1. **`import litellm`** (line 11): The module imports `litellm` but never uses it within this file. It is a dead import that causes a `ModuleNotFoundError` if litellm is not installed, even though none of the functions in gateway.py need it. This is a real issue -- it couples the module to an unnecessary runtime dependency for the functions defined here.

2. **`content_filter` -> `end_turn` mapping** (line 127): The `_FINISH_REASON_MAP` includes `"content_filter": "end_turn"`, which was not in the spec. This is not harmful but is an unrequested addition.

3. **`stop_sequence: None`** (line 165): The response includes a `stop_sequence` field set to `None`, which was not mentioned in the spec. This is harmless and matches actual Anthropic response shape, so it is acceptable.

4. **Empty content fallback** (line 163): When no content blocks are generated, the code returns `[{"type": "text", "text": ""}]`. This is a defensive measure not in the spec but reasonable.

## Summary

**Verdict: Issues Found**

1. **Unused `import litellm`** at `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/app/gateway.py:11` -- litellm is imported but never used in this file. This creates a hard runtime dependency that prevents the module from loading without litellm installed, even though none of the three functions need it. The tests themselves proved this: they failed to collect until litellm was manually installed.

2. **Missing test for malformed JSON handling** -- The spec explicitly requires "Handle malformed JSON in tool arguments gracefully." The implementation handles it (lines 145-148, wrapping into `{"raw": ...}`), but no test in `test_gateway.py` exercises this code path. The implementer claimed 15 tests "all passing" but omitted this important edge case.

3. **Minor: `startswith("claude")` vs `claude-*` prefix** at line 21 -- The check matches any model starting with `claude` (e.g., `claudeX`) rather than specifically `claude-` with a hyphen. This is a low-severity discrepancy with the spec's `claude-*` prefix requirement.

4. **Extra unrequested feature:** `content_filter` -> `end_turn` mapping at line 127 was not in the spec.

---

## Code Review: Claude Gateway Core Implementation (Tasks 2-4)

**Files reviewed:**
- `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/app/gateway.py` (170 lines, new file)
- `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/tests/test_gateway.py` (263 lines, new file)

**Plan reference:** `docs/plans/2026-02-26-claude-gateway-design.md` (commit `78333b7f`)

**Test results:** All 17 tests pass.

---

### What was done well

1. **Clean separation of concerns.** The translation functions are pure (no I/O, no state), making them easy to test and reason about. The `_convert_system_to_openai` and `_convert_message_to_openai` helpers keep `translate_request_to_openai` focused on orchestration rather than content type dispatch.

2. **Defensive handling of malformed tool arguments.** The `try/except` around `json.loads(func["arguments"])` with a `{"raw": ...}` fallback at line 146-148 of `gateway.py` is a practical safety net for real-world OpenAI responses that occasionally return invalid JSON in streaming tool calls that get concatenated.

3. **Correct tool use round-trip.** The assistant message with tool_use blocks correctly translates the `id`, `name`, and serialized `input` to OpenAI's `tool_calls` format, and tool_result blocks correctly map `tool_use_id` to `tool_call_id`. This is the most error-prone part of cross-API translation, and it is handled correctly.

4. **Good test coverage for the happy paths.** The tests cover: model detection for multiple providers, system prompt as string and list, tool definitions, tool use messages with both assistant and tool_result sides, response with text, response with tool calls, finish reason mapping, and malformed JSON recovery.

---

### Plan Alignment

The implementation aligns well with the design document's specification for Tasks 2-4:

- **Task 2 (model detection):** Matches the plan's `is_anthropic_model` spec exactly -- `claude-*`, short aliases `{haiku, sonnet, opus}`, everything else is non-Anthropic.
- **Task 3 (request translation):** Covers system prompt, messages, tools, max_tokens, and the tool_use/tool_result round-trip as specified.
- **Task 4 (response translation):** Covers text blocks, tool_use blocks, stop_reason mapping, and usage token translation as specified.

**One notable plan deviation:** The design document says the module will be ~250 lines. The current implementation is 170 lines, but this is because the non-streaming handler (`litellm.acompletion` call) and streaming handler are not yet in this file -- they are planned for Tasks 5 and 6 respectively. This is a reasonable incremental approach, not a problem.

---

### Issues

#### Critical (must fix)

**None.**

#### Important (should fix)

**1. Unused `import litellm` at line 11 of `gateway.py`.**

The `litellm` module is imported but never referenced anywhere in the current code. The translation functions are pure dict-to-dict transformations that do not use LiteLLM. This import forces the `litellm` package (which is large and has many transitive dependencies) to load on module import, adding startup latency even when only the translation helpers are needed.

This should either be removed now and re-added when Tasks 5/6 introduce `litellm.acompletion()`, or converted to a lazy import inside the future handler function.

```python
# gateway.py line 11 -- remove this line for now:
import litellm  # <-- unused, adds ~2s import overhead
```

**2. `is_anthropic_model("")` returns `True` but `is_anthropic_model(None)` will crash.**

At line 18 of `gateway.py`:
```python
def is_anthropic_model(model: str) -> bool:
    if not model:
        return True
```

The type hint says `str`, but in practice the caller will be extracting `model` from a request body dict via `body.get("model", "")`. If `body.get("model")` returns `None` (key present with null value), then `not model` is `True` and it returns `True` safely. However, the subsequent code path with `model.lower().strip()` would crash if the `not model` guard were ever removed or bypassed. The function signature should either explicitly handle `None` via `model: str | None` or the docstring should note the `None`-safe behavior. This is minor but worth tightening since the design document shows the call site as `body.get("model", "")` which could return `None` if the JSON field is literally `null`.

**3. `content_filter` maps to `end_turn` in `_FINISH_REASON_MAP` (line 128).**

```python
_FINISH_REASON_MAP = {
    ...
    "content_filter": "end_turn",
}
```

Mapping `content_filter` to `end_turn` silently hides content moderation events from the consuming SDK/agent. The Anthropic API does not have a direct equivalent, but returning `end_turn` makes it look like the model naturally stopped, when in fact the provider refused to generate. Consider either logging a warning when this mapping fires, or preserving the signal in some way (e.g., appending a text block that says "[Response filtered by provider]"). At minimum, add a code comment explaining the trade-off.

#### Suggestions (nice to have)

**4. Missing test for `None` model value.**

Add a test to verify `is_anthropic_model(None)` behaves as expected, since request bodies can contain `"model": null`:

```python
def test_none_model_is_anthropic_default():
    # model field could be JSON null
    assert is_anthropic_model(None) is True
```

**5. Missing test for tool_result with list content.**

The code at lines 49-52 of `gateway.py` handles `tool_result` where the `content` field is a list of text blocks:
```python
if isinstance(tr_content, list):
    tr_content = "\n".join(
        b.get("text", "") for b in tr_content if b.get("type") == "text"
    )
```

But no test exercises this branch. A test like this would close the gap:

```python
def test_translate_tool_result_with_list_content():
    anthropic_body = {
        "model": "gpt-4o",
        "max_tokens": 1024,
        "messages": [
            {
                "role": "user",
                "content": [
                    {
                        "type": "tool_result",
                        "tool_use_id": "tool_xyz",
                        "content": [
                            {"type": "text", "text": "Row 1: OK"},
                            {"type": "text", "text": "Row 2: OK"},
                        ],
                    }
                ],
            }
        ],
    }
    result = translate_request_to_openai(anthropic_body)
    tool_msg = result["messages"][0]
    assert tool_msg["role"] == "tool"
    assert tool_msg["content"] == "Row 1: OK\nRow 2: OK"
```

**6. Missing test for multiple tool calls in a single response.**

The code iterates `tool_calls` with a for loop (line 143: `for tc in tool_calls:`), but the test `test_translate_tool_call_response` only includes a single tool call. A test with 2+ tool calls would verify the iteration behavior.

**7. Missing test for `translate_response_to_anthropic` when `content` is `None` (no text, no tool_calls).**

When `message.get("content")` is `None` and `message.get("tool_calls", [])` is empty, the code falls back to `[{"type": "text", "text": ""}]` at line 163. This is a good fallback, but it is untested.

**8. The `_convert_message_to_openai` function has a subtle ordering assumption.**

At line 44, `tool_results` are checked first. If a message contained both `tool_result` and `tool_use` blocks (which would be malformed but possible), only the tool_results would be processed. This is likely fine in practice, but a comment noting the priority would help future maintainers.

**9. Consider adding a brief docstring to the private helpers.**

`_convert_system_to_openai` and `_convert_message_to_openai` handle non-trivial format translation logic but have no docstrings. Even a one-line summary would improve readability, for example:

```python
def _convert_message_to_openai(msg: dict) -> list[dict]:
    """Convert an Anthropic message dict to a list of OpenAI message dicts."""
```

**10. The `pyproject.toml` test configuration section is missing.**

Looking at the existing test patterns (e.g., `test_proxy.py`, `test_dependencies.py`), the test file follows the project's conventions -- plain `pytest` functions, no fixtures, no async. The test file does not have an `__init__.py` marker though -- however, neither do the other test files seem to need one as pytest discovers them by convention. This is fine.

---

### Summary

The implementation is solid, well-structured, and faithfully implements Tasks 2-4 from the design plan. The pure-function approach to request/response translation is the right architecture -- it keeps translation logic testable and decoupled from the I/O layer that will be added in Tasks 5-6.

The two **Important** items to address before moving forward:
1. Remove the unused `import litellm` (line 11 of `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/app/gateway.py`) to avoid unnecessary import overhead.
2. Consider tightening the `is_anthropic_model` signature to `model: str | None` or adding a guard comment, since the caller could pass `None`.

The **Suggestion** items -- particularly adding tests for the list-content tool_result branch (#5) and multiple tool calls (#6) -- would strengthen coverage for the most complex and error-prone code paths in the translation layer.

Added `gateway_handler(anthropic_body, stream)` (lines 174-185) which:

2. For non-streaming (`stream=False`): calls `litellm.acompletion`, converts the response with `translate_response_to_anthropic`, and returns a `JSONResponse`
3. For streaming (`stream=True`): delegates to `_handle_streaming`

Added the `from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse, StreamingResponse` import (line 12).

**Test: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/tests/test_gateway.py`**

`test_gateway_handler_non_streaming` (lines 337-369): Mocks `litellm.acompletion` to return a `MagicMock` with a canned `model_dump()`. Verifies the response is a 200 JSONResponse with correct Anthropic message format (`type`, `content[0].text`, `stop_reason`).

**Commit:** `2ded903a feat(gateway): add non-streaming gateway handler`

### Task 6: Streaming handler -- DONE

**File: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/app/gateway.py`**

Added `_handle_streaming(openai_req, model)` (lines 188-336) which:
1. Sets `stream=True` and `stream_options={"include_usage": True}` on the OpenAI request
2. Calls `litellm.acompletion` to get an async stream
3. Returns a `StreamingResponse` wrapping an async generator that translates OpenAI streaming chunks into Anthropic SSE events:
   - `message_start` -- emitted first with message metadata
   - `content_block_start` / `content_block_delta` / `content_block_stop` -- for text and tool_use blocks
   - `message_delta` -- with stop_reason and usage
   - `message_stop` -- final event
4. Properly handles tool call streaming: tracks `active_tool_calls` by index, closes text blocks before opening tool blocks, emits `input_json_delta` for incremental argument chunks

Added `_sse(event_type, data)` helper (lines 339-341) to format SSE lines.

**Test: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/tests/test_gateway.py`**

`test_gateway_handler_streaming` (lines 372-430): Creates 3 mock chunks (two content deltas + one finish), patches `litellm.acompletion` to return an async generator. Verifies the response is a `StreamingResponse` with `text/event-stream` media type. Parses all SSE events from the body iterator and checks that `message_start`, `content_block_start`, `content_block_delta`, and `message_stop` event types are all present.

**Commit:** `9135cc3f feat(gateway): add streaming handler with SSE translation`

---

### Self-review observations

1. **Correctness**: Both tests pass. The full test suite (22 tests) remains green.
2. **TDD discipline**: Tests were written first and confirmed to fail before implementation in both cases.
3. **Streaming completeness**: The streaming handler emits the full Anthropic SSE lifecycle (`message_start` -> `content_block_start` -> `content_block_delta` -> `content_block_stop` -> `message_delta` -> `message_stop`) and handles both text and tool_use content blocks.
4. **Edge case handling**: The `_handle_streaming` function handles missing `tool_calls` in deltas (defaults to empty list), missing `usage` data, and properly transitions between text blocks and tool call blocks.
5. **No regressions**: All 20 pre-existing tests continue to pass alongside the 2 new ones.

### Test Coverage for Tasks 5 & 6

| Test | What it covers |
|---|---|
| `test_gateway_handler_non_streaming` (line 338) | Mocks `litellm.acompletion`, verifies `JSONResponse` with status 200, correct body structure |
| `test_gateway_handler_streaming` (line 373) | Mocks async stream with 3 chunks (text + finish), verifies `StreamingResponse`, `text/event-stream` media type, and presence of all key SSE event types (`message_start`, `content_block_start`, `content_block_delta`, `message_stop`) |

---

### Minor Observations (not spec violations, but worth noting)

1. **`message_delta` not verified in streaming test**: The streaming test (line 426-430) checks for `message_start`, `content_block_start`, `content_block_delta`, and `message_stop` in the event types, but does not assert `message_delta` is present. The `message_delta` event IS emitted by the code (line 322-332) and would appear in the output, but the test does not explicitly verify it. This is a test coverage gap, not a code bug.

2. **No streaming test for tool calls**: The streaming test only exercises text content deltas. There is no test for the tool call streaming path (lines 258-307), which is a significant untested code path. Again, this is a coverage gap -- the implementation looks correct by inspection.

3. **`input_tokens` tracked but only `output_tokens` emitted in `message_delta`**: In the `message_delta` event (line 330), only `output_tokens` is included in `usage`. The `input_tokens` variable (line 199, updated line 226) is tracked but never emitted after `message_start`. This matches the Anthropic streaming protocol (where `input_tokens` is in `message_start` and `output_tokens` is in `message_delta`), but the `message_start` event always emits `input_tokens: 0` (line 215) rather than the actual value. This is because usage data typically arrives in the final chunk, after `message_start` has already been emitted. This is a known limitation of the streaming format and matches how the Anthropic SDK itself behaves.

4. **`tool_calls` default to empty list**: On line 259, `delta.get("tool_calls", [])` -- some providers may return `None` instead of omitting the key, which would bypass the default and cause `for tc in None` to fail. A safer pattern would be `delta.get("tool_calls") or []`.

---

### Commit Count Verification

The implementer claims 2 commits. The git log shows:
- `9135cc3f feat(gateway): add streaming handler with SSE translation` (Task 6)
- `2ded903a feat(gateway): add non-streaming gateway handler` (Task 5)

This is correct -- 2 commits for Tasks 5 and 6.

### Test Count Verification

The implementer claims 22 tests all passing. Confirmed: 22 tests collected, 22 passed.

---

## Verdict

**PASS -- Spec compliant**, with minor observations:

1. **Test gap**: `message_delta` event type is not asserted in the streaming test.
2. **Test gap**: No test exercises the streaming tool call path (`content_block_start` with `tool_use` + `input_json_delta` deltas).
3. **Potential runtime issue**: `delta.get("tool_calls", [])` on line 259 would fail if the provider returns `None` for `tool_calls` instead of omitting the key. Should be `delta.get("tool_calls") or []`.

None of these are spec violations -- the implementation faithfully implements all specified behaviors. The observations are about defensive coding and test thoroughness.

### Task 7: Route non-Anthropic models to gateway (`4207a4f4`)

**File: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/app/proxy.py`**

Changes:
- Added `import json as json_mod` and `from app.gateway import is_anthropic_model, gateway_handler`
- Moved `body = await request.body()` above the existing upstream forwarding block so the body is read once and reused
- Inserted gateway routing logic: for `POST v1/messages`, the body is parsed as JSON, the `model` field is checked via `is_anthropic_model()`, and non-Anthropic models are routed to `gateway_handler()` with the `stream` flag extracted from the body

**Tests added (`test_proxy.py`):**
- `test_proxy_routes_non_anthropic_model_to_gateway` -- confirms `gpt-4o` is routed to `gateway_handler`
- `test_proxy_still_forwards_anthropic_model_upstream` -- confirms `claude-sonnet-4-6` does NOT call `gateway_handler`

### Task 8: Augment `/v1/models` with non-Anthropic models (`e6372246`)

**File: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/app/proxy.py`**

Changes:
- Updated config import to include `ANTHROPIC_MODEL, SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL, CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL`
- Added `_get_configured_non_anthropic_models()` -- collects non-Anthropic model names from the three config variables
- Added `_augmented_models_response()` -- forwards `GET /v1/models` to Anthropic upstream, then appends non-Anthropic model entries with `id`, `type`, `display_name`, and `created_at` fields, deduplicating against existing IDs
- Inserted intercept in `proxy_anthropic` for `GET v1/models`: if there are configured non-Anthropic models, returns the augmented response instead of the plain upstream pass-through

**Test added (`test_proxy.py`):**
- `test_models_endpoint_includes_non_anthropic_models` -- mocks the upstream response and `_get_configured_non_anthropic_models`, verifies that `gpt-4o`, `gemini/gemini-2.0-flash`, and `claude-sonnet-4-6` all appear in the returned model list

### Self-review notes

- The request body is read once (`body = await request.body()`) and reused for both gateway routing and upstream forwarding, avoiding the "body already consumed" issue.
- JSON parse errors in the gateway routing path fall back to an empty dict, which means `model` defaults to `""`, which `is_anthropic_model("")` treats as Anthropic (returns True), so malformed requests correctly fall through to the upstream Anthropic path.
- The `/v1/models` augmentation only activates when there are actually configured non-Anthropic models; otherwise the request falls through to the normal upstream forwarding.
- The `_augmented_models_response` deduplicates by checking `existing_ids` before appending.
- All 37 proxy + gateway tests pass. The failures in the full suite are pre-existing sandbox permission issues unrelated to these changes.

## Report: Spec Compliance Review for `proxy.py` (Tasks 7 & 8)

### Task 7: Intercept `POST v1/messages` and route non-Anthropic models to gateway

**Verdict: PASS (with minor observations)**

Checklist:

1. **Intercepts `POST v1/messages`** -- Lines 145-153 check `request.method == "POST" and path == "v1/messages"`. Correct.

2. **Parses JSON body** -- Line 147 uses `json_mod.loads(body)`. Correct.

3. **Checks model with `is_anthropic_model()`** -- Line 151 calls `is_anthropic_model(model)`. Correct. The function is imported from `app.gateway` (line 11) and works as expected (returns `True` for Claude models, short aliases, and empty/None).

4. **If non-Anthropic: routes to `gateway_handler(body, stream=stream)`** -- Lines 152-153 extract `stream` from the parsed body and call `gateway_handler(body_json, stream=stream)`. The `gateway_handler` signature (line 174 of `gateway.py`) accepts `(anthropic_body: dict, stream: bool)`, matching the call. Correct.

5. **If Anthropic: falls through to existing upstream forwarding** -- Lines 155-200 handle the Anthropic case unchanged. Correct.

6. **Body is read once and reused** -- Line 144 reads `body = await request.body()` once. For the gateway path, the parsed JSON dict is passed directly. For the Anthropic upstream path, the raw `body` bytes are passed at line 175 via `content=body`. The body is never read a second time from `request`. Correct.

**Minor observation:** The `body = await request.body()` call on line 144 runs unconditionally for ALL requests (GET, DELETE, etc.), not just POST. For GET requests to non-`v1/models` paths, this is harmless (returns `b""`) but slightly wasteful. Not a spec violation.

---

### Task 8: Augment `GET /v1/models` with non-Anthropic model entries

**Verdict: PASS**

Checklist:

1. **`_get_configured_non_anthropic_models()`** (lines 75-81) -- Checks `ANTHROPIC_MODEL`, `SQL_SUBAGENT_MODEL`, `CHART_SUBAGENT_MODEL` env vars. For each, calls `is_anthropic_model()` and collects those that return `False`. Returns sorted list. Correct.

2. **`_augmented_models_response()`** (lines 84-115) -- Forwards `GET /v1/models` to Anthropic upstream, parses the response JSON, then appends synthetic entries for non-Anthropic models (avoiding duplicates via `existing_ids` check). Returns a `JSONResponse` with the augmented data. Correct.

3. **Each synthetic model entry has required fields** -- Lines 106-111 include `id`, `type` ("model"), `display_name`, and `created_at` ("2024-01-01T00:00:00Z"). All four fields present. Correct.

4. **Intercepted in `proxy_anthropic` when non-Anthropic models configured** -- Lines 134-141 check `request.method == "GET" and path == "v1/models"`, then call `_get_configured_non_anthropic_models()`. If the list is non-empty, it calls `_augmented_models_response()` and returns early. If the list IS empty, it falls through to normal Anthropic upstream forwarding. Correct.

5. **GET /v1/models is in `_UNAUTHENTICATED_PASSTHROUGH`** -- Line 72 includes `"v1/models"`, so this endpoint does not require a session token. Correct.

---

### Test Coverage (`test_proxy.py`)

| Test | What it covers |
|---|---|
| `test_proxy_routes_non_anthropic_model_to_gateway` (line 116) | POST with `model: "gpt-4o"` calls `gateway_handler` |
| `test_proxy_still_forwards_anthropic_model_upstream` (line 143) | POST with `model: "claude-sonnet-4-6"` does NOT call `gateway_handler` |
| `test_models_endpoint_includes_non_anthropic_models` (line 159) | GET `/v1/models` augments response with `gpt-4o` and `gemini/gemini-2.0-flash` alongside Claude models |

All three tests exercise the correct code paths and make appropriate assertions.

---

### Final Verdict: PASS -- Spec compliant

Both Task 7 and Task 8 are implemented correctly per the spec:

- **Task 7**: POST `v1/messages` is intercepted, JSON body is parsed, model is checked with `is_anthropic_model()`, non-Anthropic models are routed to `gateway_handler(body, stream=stream)`, Anthropic models fall through to existing upstream forwarding, and the body is read exactly once.
- **Task 8**: `_get_configured_non_anthropic_models()` collects non-Anthropic model names from the three env vars. `_augmented_models_response()` forwards to Anthropic and appends synthetic entries with all four required fields (`id`, `type`, `display_name`, `created_at`). The GET `/v1/models` interception is properly gated on non-Anthropic models being configured.

Now I have a thorough understanding of the entire implementation. Here is my review.

---

## Code Review: Claude Gateway -- Multi-Provider Model Support via LiteLLM

### Overall Assessment

This is a well-executed feature implementation. The code is clean, logically organized, and closely follows the approved design document. The commit history shows a disciplined, incremental approach (12 small commits building up functionality layer by layer). All 39 new/modified tests pass. The implementation successfully avoids touching unrelated subsystems (sidecar, frontend, agent.py, MCP tools) as planned.

**Acknowledgements -- what was done well:**

- Clean separation of concerns: `gateway.py` handles all translation logic; `proxy.py` handles routing decisions only.
- The streaming implementation in `_handle_streaming` correctly tracks content block indices, active tool calls, and emits the full Anthropic SSE event sequence (`message_start` -> `content_block_start` -> `content_block_delta` -> `content_block_stop` -> `message_delta` -> `message_stop`).
- Defensive handling of malformed tool arguments (line 146-149 of `gateway.py`) with a graceful `{"raw": ...}` fallback.
- The `_augmented_models_response` function in `proxy.py` correctly deduplicates by checking `existing_ids` before appending synthetic model entries.
- The `is_anthropic_model` function handles edge cases well: `None`, empty string, case-insensitive matching, short aliases.
- Good test coverage of both pure translation functions and integration-level handler tests.

---

### Plan Alignment Analysis

The implementation follows the design document faithfully. All planned components are present:

| Planned Item | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `is_anthropic_model()` detection | Implemented | Matches spec exactly |
| Request translation (Anthropic -> OpenAI) | Implemented | System prompt, messages, tools, tool results all handled |
| Response translation (OpenAI -> Anthropic) | Implemented | Text, tool_use, finish reasons all mapped |
| Non-streaming handler | Implemented | Via `litellm.acompletion` |
| Streaming handler with SSE translation | Implemented | Full event sequence |
| Proxy routing in `proxy.py` | Implemented | POST v1/messages intercept |
| `/v1/models` augmentation | Implemented | Forward + append |
| `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` relaxation | Implemented | Conditional warning |
| `.env.example` updates | Implemented | Provider key examples |
| `litellm` dependency | Implemented | Added to pyproject.toml |

**No deviations from the plan were found.** The design said ~250 lines for gateway.py; the implementation is 342 lines, which is reasonable given the full streaming implementation.

---

### Issues

#### Critical (must fix)

**1. No error handling in `gateway_handler` for LiteLLM exceptions**

File: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/app/gateway.py`, lines 174-185.

The `gateway_handler` function calls `litellm.acompletion()` without any try/except. If LiteLLM raises an exception (invalid API key, rate limit, provider outage, network error), the exception will propagate as an unhandled 500 Internal Server Error with a Python traceback. The design document explicitly calls out: "Provider-specific errors need translation to Anthropic error format for the SDK to handle gracefully."

The Claude Agent SDK expects errors in the Anthropic error response format:
```json
{"type": "error", "error": {"type": "api_error", "message": "..."}}
```

Recommendation:

```python
async def gateway_handler(anthropic_body: dict, stream: bool):
    """Handle a non-Anthropic model request by translating via LiteLLM."""
    model = anthropic_body["model"]
    openai_req = translate_request_to_openai(anthropic_body)

    try:
        if stream:
            return await _handle_streaming(openai_req, model)

        response = await litellm.acompletion(**openai_req)
        openai_dict = response.model_dump()
        anthropic_resp = translate_response_to_anthropic(openai_dict, model=model)
        return JSONResponse(content=anthropic_resp)
    except Exception as e:
        error_msg = str(e)
        # Map to Anthropic error format so the SDK handles it gracefully
        return JSONResponse(
            status_code=502,
            content={
                "type": "error",
                "error": {
                    "type": "api_error",
                    "message": f"Gateway error ({model}): {error_msg}",
                },
            },
        )
```

This is critical because without it, the SDK will receive a raw 500 error it cannot parse, potentially causing the entire agent session to crash rather than gracefully reporting the provider error.

---

#### Important (should fix)

**2. `_augmented_models_response` will fail when `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is empty**

File: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/app/proxy.py`, lines 84-115.

When a user configures only non-Anthropic models (the explicitly supported scenario), `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` will be empty. The `_augmented_models_response` function still attempts to call `api.anthropic.com/v1/models` with an empty API key on line 87 (`headers_copy["x-api-key"] = ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`). This will result in a 401 from Anthropic. The `try/except` on lines 96-99 will catch the JSON parse failure and fall back to `{"data": []}`, which is functional but wasteful -- it makes a guaranteed-to-fail network request on every `/v1/models` call.

Recommendation: skip the upstream call when there is no Anthropic key:

```python
async def _augmented_models_response(request, headers, body, extra_models):
    """Forward GET /v1/models to Anthropic, then append non-Anthropic model entries."""
    data = {"data": []}

    if ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:
        headers_copy = dict(headers)
        headers_copy["x-api-key"] = ANTHROPIC_API_KEY
        headers_copy["anthropic-version"] = "2023-06-01"

        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(30.0)) as client:
            upstream_resp = await client.get(
                f"{ANTHROPIC_UPSTREAM}/v1/models",
                headers=headers_copy,
            )
        try:
            data = upstream_resp.json()
        except Exception:
            data = {"data": []}

    models_list = data.get("data", [])
    # ... rest unchanged
```

**3. Streaming error handling is missing**

File: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/app/gateway.py`, lines 188-336.

The `_handle_streaming` function's `generate()` async generator has no try/except around the `async for chunk in response_stream:` loop. If LiteLLM raises during streaming (e.g., mid-stream rate limit, network disconnect), the SSE stream will terminate abruptly without emitting a `message_stop` event. The SDK will hang waiting for the stream to complete.

Recommendation: wrap the stream iteration in a try/except inside `generate()` and emit an error event on failure, or at minimum emit `message_stop` so the SDK does not hang.

**4. Circular import risk in `config.py`**

File: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/app/config.py`, line 14.

The `_any_anthropic_model_configured()` function uses a local import `from app.gateway import is_anthropic_model` to avoid a circular import at module level. This works today because `gateway.py` does not import from `config.py`. However, this is a fragile arrangement -- if `gateway.py` ever needs config values (e.g., a default timeout or provider-specific settings), the deferred import would break at runtime during module load. 

Recommendation: extract `is_anthropic_model` and the `_ANTHROPIC_SHORT_ALIASES` set into a small standalone module (e.g., `app/model_utils.py`) that has no imports from within the app. Both `config.py` and `gateway.py` can then import from it safely.

**5. `body` is read unconditionally even for GET requests**

File: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/app/proxy.py`, line 144.

After the `/v1/models` augmentation block, `body = await request.body()` is called for every request, including GET requests that already returned early above. For non-models GET requests (e.g., hypothetical future endpoints), the body is read and then `json_mod.loads(body)` is attempted unnecessarily. While not a bug (GET bodies are empty, the JSONDecodeError is caught), it adds needless overhead.

Recommendation: move `body = await request.body()` inside the POST check or guard it with a method check:

```python
body = await request.body()
if request.method == "POST" and path == "v1/messages":
```
should become:
```python
if request.method == "POST" and path == "v1/messages":
    body = await request.body()
    ...
```
and keep the existing `body = await request.body()` for the Anthropic forwarding path below, or consolidate:
```python
body = b""
if request.method == "POST" and path == "v1/messages":
    body = await request.body()
    try:
        body_json = json_mod.loads(body)
    except (json_mod.JSONDecodeError, ValueError):
        body_json = {}
    model = body_json.get("model", "")
    if not is_anthropic_model(model):
        stream = body_json.get("stream", False)
        return await gateway_handler(body_json, stream=stream)

# Existing Anthropic upstream forwarding
if not body:
    body = await request.body()
```

Actually, looking more carefully, `body` is also used below on line 175 for the Anthropic upstream forwarding path (`content=body`), so it must be read for non-gateway requests too. The current code reads it once and uses it for both paths, which is correct. The only issue is minor: for GET requests to non-models paths, it reads an empty body. This is a very minor concern.

---

#### Suggestions (nice to have)

**6. Inline import of `JSONResponse` in `_augmented_models_response`**

File: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/app/proxy.py`, line 114.

```python
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
```

This import is already available at module scope (line 7 imports `StreamingResponse` from `fastapi.responses`). Adding `JSONResponse` to the top-level import is cleaner:

```python
from fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse, JSONResponse
```

**7. `content_block_idx` is not incremented after opening the first tool call when there was no preceding text block**

File: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/app/gateway.py`, lines 260-292.

When a streaming response starts directly with a tool call (no preceding text), `has_emitted_text_block` is `False`, so the `content_block_stop` for the text block is skipped (correct), but `content_block_idx` starts at 0. The first tool call gets block index 0. If a second tool call arrives, `content_block_idx` is not incremented between tool calls that do not have an intervening text block. Looking more carefully at the code, `content_block_idx` is only incremented when a text block is stopped before a tool call starts (line 268). For consecutive tool calls without text, each new tool call in `active_tool_calls` sets its `block_idx` to the current `content_block_idx`, but `content_block_idx` is never incremented between them. This means multiple tool calls could share the same block index.

Let me trace through the logic for two consecutive tool calls:
- tc_idx=0 enters `active_tool_calls`, gets `block_idx=0`, emits `content_block_start` with index 0
- tc_idx=1 enters `active_tool_calls`, gets `block_idx=0`, emits `content_block_start` with index 0

Both tool calls would emit with index 0, which is incorrect. The Anthropic streaming spec expects each content block to have a unique, monotonically increasing index.

Recommendation: increment `content_block_idx` after emitting each tool call `content_block_start`:

```python
active_tool_calls[tc_idx] = {
    "id": tool_id,
    "name": tool_name,
    "block_idx": content_block_idx,
}

yield _sse(
    "content_block_start",
    { ... },
)
content_block_idx += 1  # Add this line
```

On reflection, this is actually more important than a "nice to have" -- with the current code, parallel tool calls in streaming mode will produce malformed Anthropic SSE events with duplicate block indices. I would elevate this to **Important** severity if multi-tool streaming is a realistic scenario for the target providers (GPT-4o does support parallel tool calls).

**8. Missing test for streaming with tool calls**

The streaming test (`test_gateway_handler_streaming`) only tests text content streaming. There is no test for streaming tool calls, which is the most complex part of the streaming logic and has the block index bug noted above.

**9. The `test_subagent_config.py` test is now too restrictive**

File: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/tests/test_subagent_config.py`, line 6-7.

The existing test asserts subagent model names must be in `("haiku", "sonnet", "opus", "inherit")`. With the gateway feature, these models can now be any LiteLLM model name (e.g., `gpt-4o-mini`, `gemini/gemini-2.0-flash`). This test was not modified and will fail in non-Anthropic configurations. Since this test was not in the PR's file list, this may be intentional (the test only runs in default config), but it is worth noting.

**10. `input_tokens` not included in `message_start` during streaming**

File: `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/app/gateway.py`, lines 202-218.

The `message_start` event always emits `"usage": {"input_tokens": 0, "output_tokens": 0}`. The actual `input_tokens` value arrives in a later chunk with `usage` data, and is captured in the `input_tokens` variable (line 226-227), but this value is only used in the never-emitted field. The `message_delta` event on line 330 emits only `output_tokens`. The `input_tokens` count is effectively lost in streaming mode. This is a minor fidelity gap -- the real Anthropic API includes `input_tokens` in the `message_start` event.

---

### Summary of Findings

| Severity | Count | Key Items |
|---|---|---|
| Critical | 1 | No error handling for LiteLLM exceptions in gateway_handler |
| Important | 4 | Empty API key hitting Anthropic on /v1/models; no streaming error handling; circular import risk; duplicate block indices for parallel tool calls in streaming |
| Suggestion | 4 | Inline import cleanup; missing streaming tool call test; overly restrictive subagent config test; lost input_tokens in streaming |

### Recommendation

Fix the critical issue (error handling) and the duplicate block index bug before merging. The remaining important items can be addressed in a fast follow-up, but the `/v1/models` empty-key issue and streaming error handling should not linger long since they affect the pure non-Anthropic configuration scenario that is a primary use case of this feature.

## Summary of Changes

### Fix 1 (Critical): Error handling in `gateway_handler`
**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/app/gateway.py`

Wrapped the `gateway_handler` function body in a `try/except` block. If the LiteLLM provider call throws an exception (e.g., invalid API key, network error), it now returns a `502 Bad Gateway` response in the Anthropic error format (`{"type": "error", "error": {"type": "api_error", "message": "..."}}`).

A new test `test_gateway_handler_error_returns_anthropic_error_format` was added in `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/tests/test_gateway.py`.

### Fix 2 (Important): Skip upstream call when `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is empty
**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/app/proxy.py`

Modified `_augmented_models_response` to guard the upstream Anthropic API call behind `if ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:`. When the key is empty (pure non-Anthropic setup), the function skips the upstream call entirely and returns only the synthetic non-Anthropic model entries. The return status code is now always `200` (no longer dependent on an upstream response that may not exist).

The existing test `test_models_endpoint_includes_non_anthropic_models` was updated to patch `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` to a non-empty value (since it tests the upstream-calling path). A new test `test_models_endpoint_skips_upstream_when_no_api_key` was added in `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/tests/test_proxy.py` to verify the skip behavior.

### Fix 3 (Important): Fix duplicate block indices for parallel tool calls in streaming
**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/app/gateway.py`

Added `content_block_idx += 1` after emitting `content_block_start` for a new tool_use block. Without this, two parallel tool calls arriving in the same stream would both get the same `content_block_idx`, causing the SDK to misinterpret the stream. The `active_tool_calls` dict already captures the correct block index before the increment, so subsequent `content_block_delta` events still reference the right block.

### Fix 4 (Important): Streaming error handling
**File:** `/Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway/backend/app/gateway.py`

Wrapped the `async for chunk in response_stream:` loop inside `generate()` with a `try/except`. On error, it emits a text content block containing the error message, followed by `content_block_stop`, `message_delta` (with `stop_reason: "end_turn"`), and `message_stop`. This ensures the SDK client always receives a properly terminated stream and does not hang.

### Test Results
All **41 tests** pass across `test_gateway.py` (23 tests), `test_proxy.py` (16 tests), and `test_config.py` (2 tests).

> AGENT

To work in the worktree, just `cd` into it:

```bash
cd /Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent/.claude/worktrees/claude-gateway
```

That directory is already on the `feature/claude-gateway` branch — no `git checkout` needed. Worktrees are separate working directories that each have their own checked-out branch.

A few useful commands:

```bash
# See all worktrees
git worktree list

# When done, remove the worktree (from the main repo)
cd /Users/cyyeh/Desktop/duckdb-data-agent
git worktree remove .claude/worktrees/claude-gateway
```
